Bombay, the 23rd February 1987 

No. UT/391/DPD (P&R) 77/Vol 1X/86-87.—Amendments 
to the provisions of the Monthly Income Unit Scheme series 
1—5 approved by the Board in the meeting held on February 
19, 1987 are published below for general information : 

1. The definition of “Unitholder 11 appearing in the pro¬ 
vision of all the Schemes 1^5 shall be amended to read as 
follows : 

“Unitholder” used as an expression under the Scheme 
shall mean and include the applicant and also any 
unitholder who has exercised an option to convert units 
held under all or any of the series, of Monthly Income 
Unit Schemes (1) to (5) currently in force. 

The following clause shall be inserted immediately after 
the clause on repurchase in the provisions of each scheme 
as clause :

“Notwithstanding anyhing to the contrary contained in the 
provisions of this Scheme a unitholder under this Scheme 
shall have an option to convert the units held by him into 
units under this Monthly Income Unit Scheme (8) 1987. 

This option to the unitholder shall be exercised only once 
and shall be in the manner determined by the Trust. 

The option fo so convert the units is irrevocable. 

Upon exercise of the option by a unitholder the Trust 
shall repurchase the units held by the unitholder under this 
Scheme at the repurchase price ruling on 30th June 1987 
and with immediate effect the unitholder or class of unit- 
holders \yho have exercised the option shall be deemed to be 
unitholders under the said Monthly Income Unit Scheme 
(8) 1987. Such unitholders shall be entitled to all the 
rights accruing to the fresh applicant under the said Scheme 
and shall be treated on par with them. The excess amount 
if any which constitutes the premium the units carry ns on 
30th June 1987 after repurchase shall be refunded to the 
unitholders.

5.0 The examination shall be held in accordance with .tho 
prescribed syllabus. 

6.0 The medium of examination shall be English. 

7. The internal assessment will be based on the record of 
day-to-day performance of the candidate in the practical classes 
and the field works. 

8.0 The number of theory papers and practical!* and dis¬ 
sertation as well as the distribution of marks among theory 
papers, practicals dissertation and viva voce if any, shall be 
given as in the syllabus. 

8.1 The minimum marks required to pass the examination 
shall be 50% in fa) each theory paper, (b) each practical 
and (c) dissertation. 

8.2 The papers will be set jointly by the external and inter¬ 
nal examiners (internal examiners is one who has actually 
taught the course). The examination in practicals will be 
held as decided by the Board of Control who will appoint 
the respective external and internal examiners in the concerned 
practical papers which shall also include the viva voce test 
in the paperfs) concerned, 

8.3 The answerbooks would be examined independently by 
the internal and external examiners The average of the two 
awards would be taken as long as the dilfcrcnce between the 
two awards is not more than 10% of the maximum marks. 
In case where the difference between tho two awards is more 
than ten percent, the scripts w'ould be sent to the third exa¬ 
miner for marking and the average of the two highest scores 
would be taken as the final score, 

8.4 A candidate who fails in the first examination may be 
allowed to reappear in the subsequent year with the maximum 
of two chances. 

8.5 A candidate who has failed in one or more theory 
papers or one or more practicals may be allowed to re-appear 
at. the subsequent examination in the respective theory and/or 
practicals paper (s). 

8.6 Every candidate shall submit his dissertation at the 
time of his first examination and latest within two months 
t>f the completion of the theory and practical examination. 

8.7 The Board of Control shall approve the reassignment 
of each candidate or his/her topic/subject of dissertation on 
the recommendation of the respective supervisor. The Super¬ 
visors for assignment/attachmcnt shall be a Faculty Member 
in Geology of the Panjab University. 

8.8 The dissertation is required to be sent to the external 
examiner, who shall be beyond the jurisdiction of Pan jab 
Universtiy and a non-resident of Chandigarh, to evaluate it 
in terms of numerical marks. 

The examiner to evaluate the thesis, as mentioned nbve. 
shall be appointed, subject/topic-wise, by tho Board of Control 
as per the recommendation of the internal Supervisor(s). 


The marks awarded by the examiners of each dissertation 
shall be moderated and decided upon at the time of compre¬ 
hensive viva voce test(s) which would be conducted by the 
Board of Examiners consisting of external examiner(s), 
Supervisors) and Head of the Department as Chairman of 
the Hoard. 

8.9 A candidate who fails to secure minimum pass marks 
in dissertation, shall be required to submit a fresh dissertation 
on a subject/topic to be assigned by the Board of Control 
in consultation with the supervisor concerned. * 

The candidate shall pay the tuition fee for the whole period 
till he gets through in this paper. 

9.0 Successful candidates who secure 70% and more of the 
aggregate marks shall be placed in ‘MEHIT GRADE' and 
the position in order of merit as also percentage of marks 
obtained by the candidates shall be mentioned in the Diploma. 
Those who secure 60% or more but less than 70% of the 
aggregate marks shall be placed in “VERY GOOD GRADE’* 
which shall be mentioned in the Diploma together with the 
percentage of marks obtained by the candidate. Those who 
secure 50% or more but less than 60% of the aggregate 
marks shall be placed in “Good Grade” which shall be men¬ 
tioned in the Diploma together with the percentage of marks 
obtained by the candidate, 

10.0 Each successful candidate shall receive a Diploma 
showing the percentage of marks and grade secured by the 
candidate as well as the lopic/themc of the dissertation. The 
Diploma shall he awarded to the candidate at the Annual 
Convocation of the University.

gible for proportionate exemption in regard to the minimum

18. BACHELOR OF ARCHITECTURE (NEW SCHEME) 

1.1 (a) The duration of the course for the degree of 
Bachelor of Architecture (B. Arch.) shall be five years divided 
into ten semesters. There shall be an examination at the 
end of each semester except in the case of seventh semester 
during which there shall be 24 weeks Practical Training. 
Ten Semesters shall further be grouped in two stages viz. 
stage I from first to sixth semester and stage II from seventh 
to tenth semester. 

(b) Only those persons who have secured 60 per cent or 
more aggregate marks in all tho subjects prescribed for first 
stage of the course shall be permitted to continue their studies 
at the second stage of the course. Those eligible to take 
up this second stage shall have the option of completing it 
at a stretch or in parts, but within five years from the 
completion of the first stage. Those who fail to complete the 
second stage of the course with in the stipulated period shall 
be considered unsuitable for the award of the degree of 
Bachelor of Architecture (B. Arch). 

1.2 The Semesters examination shall ordinarily be held in 
the months of December and May, on such dates as may be 
fixed by the Syndicate. 

1.3 The last date for receipt of examination admissions 
forms and fees' without late fees and with late fees of Rs. 5/- 
as fixed by the Syndicate shall be notified by the Registrar. 

Provided that the candidates shall have to pay an extra 
late fee of Rs, 50/- after the stipulated last date with a 
late fee of Rs. 5/- is over. No candidate shall be permitted 
to deposit extra late fee directly with the University. 

2. A person who has passed one of the following examina¬ 
tions with at least 55 per cent marks in the aggregate of

after graduation.

of April or on such, dates as may be fixed by the Syndicate.

lectures delivered.

to 10 per cent in each paper may be condoned by the

tion.

fc) One third of the Readers not exceeding two by

covered under (b) and (c).

Chancellor.

laid down in the syllabus published from time to time.

English.

nation shall be :
